# Minagho — round 2 set-piece sheet

## Implementation addendum (round 2)

The planning record below is retained as supplied. `storylines/minagho_round2.py`
implements the route-local situations after the existing stance module; its
Trickster integrator is the only changed CRLF source file. The fixed reservation
is copied to `tools/route_packs/turning_points.json` under `minagho` only.

- SP1/2, MC-01/03: the offer remains an enemy's useful opening; the actual paid
  transfer gets physical proof. The existing collector receipt carries her
  deliberate refusal to finish the hunt. No additional seal effect is claimed.
- SP3/4, MC-02/04/05/06: she intercepts Chivarro's dagger and retains the debtor;
  expulsion gets both women's answer. The public bloody-hand reckoning carries
  three accounts of the same price, then her independent rare-visit claim. The
  price letter now stages that reckoning; the house still has an ordinary door.
- SP5/6: existing near-discovery, stance, discovery, refusal and aftermath paths
  remain; the eight repeated clinical absent-partner lines are rewritten as her
  appraisal. All 46 supplied briefs have append-only dedicated heated-cut nodes.
  Existing ending exits retain their effects. No explicit prose is generated.
- TP F21--24: all four killer-history solo copies offer the same purchase/burial
  sequence inside their existing scene. The new receipt is produced after her
  burial, and gates the solo accepting answers and Minagho ending. A scar does
  not settle this debt. The purchase is narrated at Herrax's unquantified asking
  price; no invented currency amount or fake native inventory binding is added.
- TP P17--19/F33--35: the purse is prepared before dispatch, the abandoned purse
  is refunded, and physical/secret letter-night alternatives pay the existing
  100 rent once. P27--33's five shared-clock letters use 120 hours; the two solo
  letters use 192. Legal Chapter-5 readiness and the combined delivery budget
  still need shared pacing work; these delays alone do not prove that budget.
- TP P22--26/F39--41: local meeting variants distinguish an already earned
  Trickster reunion from the ordinary journey. The second final-arrangement
  page is locked after the Trickster commitment. The own Last Call paragraph
  accepts `future_together`; the solo coda shows invited returns and the palm.
- TP F52--53: the Commander answers Minagho's threat with principled, pragmatic
  or complicit words. A plain robe is established before the later night.

## Reservation, voice and scope

**Registered device:** `canon_lowest_point`, Ch5 Drezen quartermaster stores after joint brand reckoning. **Registered payoff:** branded debtor makes her own rare-visit claim and binds the bleeding palm at dawn. Her grabbing the hand is the decision's expression, not a second pursuit device. Brand transfer, collateral purchase, wardrobe passage and Chivarro's deposit remain access devices. The page does not choose affection; Minagho does.

Avoid the eight-route lowest-point cluster's interchangeable rescue/gratitude: Soana, Gesmerha, Nurah, Dorgelinda, Hepzamirah, Kaylessa and Jannah do not share this mark, debtor or partner. Avoid Chivarro's private rent-counteroffer as Minagho's pivot, Herrax's rival intervention, Kiana's mid-act husband interruption, generic public confession, ritual-induced desire, intoxication as a shortcut, oath/token at a farewell, and a new forged record. MC-04 uses an already genuine independent copy, never another false proof. No echo slot is allocated to Minagho: none is proposed. Shyka may only condition a separately earned fate device where already assigned, never pay a romance debt or tell her why she wants someone.

Minagho's voice: wounded arrogance; contempt for paladins and convenient mercy; short commands; amused malice; carnal possessiveness; vicious memory. She can care for Chivarro and the Commander while enjoying suffering and remaining the butcher of Kenabres. Chivarro's voice: price, appetite, a madam's exact knowledge of who wants what, profanity when cheated, jealous pride. Neither gives an ethics lecture or narrates a healthy arrangement. Refusals and willing participation appear in actions and player answers. Existing service branches retain their coercive character and are not turned into romantic slots.

This sheet stages existing costs and audit-named debts. It adds no fee, score, attraction threshold, reconciliation condition, mandatory sidequest, immunity or new gate. All shared implementation and Chivarro-owner changes require coordination. Nothing here authorizes source edits.

## Six set pieces

### 1. The enemy names the wound — entry across Ch2 and Ch4

**Where/when:** Ch2 Drezen citadel hostile encounter (`longcon.citadel_offer`, read only); Ch4 Minagho's hideout after her own brand recital (`T.minagho_dead.setup_c4`, where `T = minagho_chivarro.trickster.`). **Present:** Commander, Minagho, only the native encounter's actually present company. Chivarro is elsewhere. **Risk:** Minagho is still an enemy; the crusade has been attacked, and Baphomet punishes failure. Helping her escape does not resolve her hostility.

**Lead-in and beats:** The Ch2 opening offers the existing con while her humiliation is fresh. Keep every Bluff provenance and her native exit; hold her attention, never meet nonexistent eyes. In Ch4 she snarls the blood condition herself. The Commander can volunteer the existing debt or leave the native kill/spare conversation intact. Her counter-move is to hear the literal claim, mock the mortal toy, and reserve the option of selling that name to Baphomet. No kiss, reward or gratitude at the wound. Her facial mutilation is neither prettified nor eroticized.

**Decision/options/payoff:** She decides whether the stranger's offer is useful, not whether she loves them. Existing preparation and native fate answers keep their identities. The early offer gives her a specific offender and possible debtor to recall in Ch5. Explicit player execution and user-ruled closures stand; no return is owed for those histories.

**Carry:** Long Con callbacks stay history-specific; dead/spared/fled states remain distinct. No household, lover or Last Call body is earned here. **Canon hooks:** C1–C4; source's native Long Con binding is retained. The Azata Fane cue is a characterization anchor only; its inaccessible Trickster primer is an escalation, not a relocated encounter.

### 2. Prove what the seal actually does — paid access, Ch5

**Where/when:** Existing Ivory Labyrinth parley and subsequent quartermaster-store transfer/collector scenes: `T.react.baphomet`; `T.minagho_dead.{brand,brand_letter,collateral}` or `T.spared.{brand,brand_letter}`; `T.debt.collectors{,_spared}`. **Present:** Baphomet only at his parley; Commander, Minagho if alive or legitimately restored; his existing servants where already staged; Wilcer on the Drezen side. **Risk:** collateral, the Commander's bleeding palm, the existing Favors losses and harsher collectors' iron. The war and a demon lord's claim persist after the trick.

**Lead-in and beats:** MC-01 staged proof is folded into the already paid transfer: show her brow stop bleeding while the volunteered palm opens, before anyone claims the trick worked. A minor physical proof of the *existing* effect replaces a new seal power. A corpse is inert until the paid knife-and-brand action restores life; never sensualize the corpse or turn revival into a sexual invitation. For living Minagho she tests the known limit on the hand and sees it still reopen; she retains the mark. Baphomet's bargain is a purchase in his presence, not a forged liberation document.

MC-03 belongs in the existing collectors/receipt continuation: after testing the hand, she deliberately leaves the hunt Baphomet ordered unfinished, with the living Commander within reach. The next dawn's existing bleeding proves that payment still runs on the debtor; her refusal is not a new magical trigger. Chivarro, **if currently present**, chooses the front-door address in the existing house beat instead of another exposed closet passage. Do not invent a failure detector, a new hunt, fake execution, damage resource, transferable extra seals or blanket immunity. A more literal decoy-enforcement operation would need mechanics beyond the present findings and is escalated, not designed as an extra quest here.

**Decision/options/payoff:** Minagho elects to stay and inspect the thief's hand, or sells/rejects the opening in her existing terms. Commander pays the existing lawful road, refuses, or takes the already available harsher road; no new payment. Aid earns her presence and a dangerous conversational opportunity only.

**Carry:** exact kneeling/late-claim/iron/scar history; transferred versus wet seal; her prohibition on future bargaining; Chivarro's current whereabouts. Wilcer stops treating the hand as a harmless joke. Last Call recalls the debt, not a generic rescue. **Canon hooks:** C1, C3–C4 and cited parley hub; all relief and return readings are labelled authored. Contradictory native pain cues need only the earned Trickster-scoped alternate, leaving native versions elsewhere.

### 3. Chivarro wants the debtor dead — reunion and counter-move

**Where/when:** Ch5 existing `T.reunion.wardrobe` or `T.chivarro_dead.bought` and bill scene, with failed-presence/letter siblings. Continuation's `minachiv.her_own_arrival` is a subsequent meeting after a Trickster arrival, not a second arrival. **Present:** both women only after actual arrivals; Commander. Herrax appears only on the existing trade side and from her own profitable hostility. **Risk:** Chivarro has lost her house and resents the Commander who ruined Minagho; a service deed is not love; a historic return does not override a later departure.

**Lead-in and beats:** Chivarro's grievance arrives before her flirtation. She has her dagger and proposes killing the cause of their trouble (MC-02). Minagho does not become a peacemaker: she physically intercepts the dagger hand, explains that cutting this debtor's throat would give the Goat the prize, and wants the debtor alive for her own claim. Chivarro counters by barring the Commander from the lovers' reunion and taking Minagho's side of the bed. Their kiss is hungry, interrupted by a fresh insult; it is not a reconciliation pledge to the Commander. C6 is their remembered history, not a reenacted assault becoming Commander sex.

**Decision/options/payoff:** Commander burns the existing ownership bill or keeps a commercial/service outcome; accepts the exclusion, uses the existing invitation later, or leaves. Minagho chooses to keep both her lover and the useful debtor alive. Chivarro permits renewed contact on her terms or withholds it; she is not purchased into affection. Herrax's CHI-02 counter-move remains a hostile rival's attempt to retain customers and leverage; she gains no benevolent business alliance. No new transfer of employees or new rival encounter.

**Carry:** bill burned/kept, deposit/substitution or living wardrobe provenance, Herrax's existing favor, exclusions and departure. A solo visit to either woman is not their breakup. When one is distant she answers in her own letter; unknown is not dead; unprepared killing remains actual death. **Canon hooks:** C5–C9. The wardrobe indignity and shop-lodging are authored. The inspected stance return nodes already dramatize the arriving partner's answer; deepen those instead of another reunion device.

### 4. Put the bloody hand on the table — registered turning point

**Where/when:** Ch5 quartermaster stores after the existing name-price and house terms, staged across `T.after.the_price_of_her_name{,_letter}` and `T.after.before_the_last_road{,_letter}`. Existing `T.after.won_back` handles its own already earned walkout history. **Present:** Commander and Minagho; Chivarro if currently present, otherwise her actual letter or no answer. Wilcer at his stores is an uncomfortable witness, not a counselor. **Risk:** Minagho may be treated as conquered stock again; Chivarro may be offered a half-pair; the bloody palm is a cost in a crusader capital which remembers Kenabres.

**Lead-in and beats:** Finish the existing public name-price first. Differentiate an asserted creditor from a carrier who never claimed ownership. Chivarro makes the offender expose the hand; Minagho chooses whether to seize it. She presses the wound instead of inviting admiration, calls the debtor useful, and makes the rare-visit offer herself. Her answer is a desired claim laid over Baphomet's cost, not debt forgiven by a maxim about freedom. Chivarro answers with her own claim on Minagho before the Commander replies.

MC-04: let her savor the arrogant public reversal, but the Commander's independent copy is the already existing genuine correspondence, never an invented second forged proof or new public bout. MC-05: three purses can be three **props/accounts of the same existing transaction** laid beside the hand—what the Commander says was paid, what Chivarro says she collected, what Minagho says she stole. Do not triple-charge or award loot. Each tells a selfish version and invites the Commander differently. Chivarro's separate bargain pivot belongs to her owner; Minagho's decisive action is the hand claim. MC-06: burn the existing patron/marilith offer as chosen, and name the house in the existing house-letter beat. A burned dispatch never cancels Baphomet's seal or grants freedom by itself.

**Decision/options/payoff:** preserve accepting both, existing solo offer, soft deferral/scar retry and final dismissal. At the existing commitment the Commander can share, demand exclusivity, or propose a secret affair. Each woman answers before a night; rescue and money never answer for her. Minagho currently refuses to abandon Chivarro and can continue if the Commander backs down to the offered sharing terms; insistence closes/cools as already written. Do not invent a compliant breakup just to keep a yes everywhere. Secret is an informed, reckless night, not exclusive commitment under another name.

**Carry:** existing complete/future/committed receipts only at their actual accepting answers; stance and current partner state, no kiss-derived arrivals. She refuses another bargain over her body when the Goat later calls. Pair and solo mornings bind the palm only when it opens. **Canon hooks:** C1–C7; local public price makes wounded pride, cruelty and the partner conflict inseparable. This is the atlas reservation, not a bathhouse confession or rescue climax.

### 5. The house hears the secret — intimacy and five discovery stages

**Where/when:** Ch5 existing gaming room/private lodging and invitation chain; `minachiv.the_unhired_evening`, `minachiv.a_room_she_likes`, `minachiv.after_the_last_lamp`, and the actual stance-night/morning nodes on existing commitment scenes. **Present:** Commander and the chosen woman; the other lover only on her actual arrival, shared night or discovery. **Risk:** jealousy, access to the women's house, Herrax learning an exploitable quarrel, and their independent attachment. A night can precede the commercial plot's completion; it does not settle it.

1. **Near-discovery:** fold into existing room/waiting passages. Chivarro is due back with the game stones; Minagho leaves a cup, glove or lover's scent where it can be noticed. She hears the stair and keeps the Commander still with a hand on the collar, then decides whether to call Chivarro in or conceal this one visit. No mid-act spouse enters, no disguise-kiss to distract a husband, no new detection roll. The successful honest solo invitation keeps the partner's known hour rather than staging false cheating.
2. **Discovery:** use existing dawn/clothes/key nodes, not a new intrusion. Chivarro sees the wrong clothes and Minagho's hand on the Commander's waist; with Chivarro as lover, Minagho's dagger fixes the discarded belt to the door. When distant, the lover herself deliberately sends the night's description/receipt. A letter proves the affair, not the sender's physical arrival. Unknown whereabouts stay unresolved until an existing earned arrival.
3. **Confrontation:** Chivarro wants her key back and says she was made last to know in her own house. Minagho's anger targets the assumption that she should be grateful for secrecy. The guilty lover admits she enjoyed what the other did not know. Preserve the cruelty of that admission; neither labels it an addiction or explains trust in therapeutic terms.
4. **Stance demand and earned answer:** the demand occurs at the existing commitment; discovery cashes it out. Sharing means both women's independent answer, including a partner returning later. Exclusive means her canon-based refusal and the Commander's backdown/insistence. Secret means the existing affair and fallout. These are separate selectable histories; no second compulsory negotiation or new reconciliation gate. Death gets memory, captivity gets the cellar, silence gets uncertainty, never substitute consent.
5. **Partner's own move:** Chivarro takes the key and keeps Minagho; Minagho lets the Commander go. On the inverse affair Minagho cuts the belt, Chivarro throws back the shirt and chooses Minagho. They retain each other, their own house and continuing quarrels. Herrax's potential use of the scandal matters even without the Commander in the room. CHI-03's next-day quarrel about telling Minagho first attaches to the existing shared dawn; its cost is already settled invitation/stance fallout, not a new jealousy meter.

**Heat/payoff:** use the explicit-slot manifest below at willing private thresholds. Build-up is mouths, teeth, loosened clothes and appetite, then a heated cut; no explicit prose is authored here. Only one first night in the run. Subsequent scenes acknowledge an earlier night and deepen it; no identical first-time dialogue after a letter fallback. Wine may flavor the evening; do not add intoxication or a spell to manufacture desire. Dominance is chosen in the answer and action, not inferred from rescue, captivity or an owned bill. No sexual slot on `service`, kept-bill threats, revival, or coercive payment.

**Carry:** existing secret/exposed/cooled/closed flags and future arbitration. No new forgiveness or automatic post-discovery bed. The women can still desire one another after shutting the Commander out; no romance requires another roster woman to leave. **Canon hooks:** C5–C8, especially C6's morning recognition and continued murderous quarrels. The independent women, their ledger and keys make this theirs.

### 6. She binds it at dawn, then returns — aftermath and finale

**Where/when:** existing shared/solo dawns and scar-retry mornings in Ch5; Threshold departure, Last Call and postwar house invitations. **Present:** only those who actually spent that night there; store scenes can include Wilcer; no distant partner bodily cameo. **Risk:** seal collection, next battle, renewed manipulation, lover loss and the Commander's actual survival.

**Lead-in and beats:** Minagho waits for the palm to open, gets the bandage first, and binds it too tightly. Chivarro complains that the debtor made Minagho laugh, then quarrels over who was told what first. Minagho does not apologize for Kenabres because she had a good night. She keeps her brow dry only with the actual transfer. Keep the old scar road's five dawns and its witness; it changes the displayed wound, not the demon lord's contract. Do not add another self-harm test or present pain as a purchase of affection.

On the unprepared Chivarro-killing history, grief cannot be bought with a scar. The TP sheet/audit names the missing rings-and-burial debt: a single voluntary retrieval/payment/burial situation with Herrax's native trade can collect it, but its payment representation and source integration are escalated. Rings identity is authored, not a native inventory item. Never revive the woman deliberately killed without preparation, never make her death an eligibility price. The existing killer route's grievance must not disappear in a sensual threshold or annual summary.

**Decision/options/payoff:** retain morning flinch/verdict, hand-binding, bill/receipt and exit options. Her physical care has an edge: she assesses what the debtor can bear and wants this person back. Separate nights continue to leave the other lover's bond intact. Late epilogue invitations carry the same dawn consequence before their yearly summary. At Last Call the debt is called in according to actual kneeling/iron/assistance history, and Minagho refuses to stand beside a Commander in rooms invoking the Goat if that history says so.

**Carry:** rare invited visits, never automatic attendance every dawn. Show a concrete return: she opens the door on the next invited visit and takes the same hand; Chivarro has kept the room, raised her own price and still wants Minagho. Shared household text observes their own house/door and both women's current states. Refused/secret-exposed outcomes get no new lover night. Unreturned dead Commander gets remembrance only; Last Call's survival device must actually have applied before a living postwar visit. **Canon hooks:** C1, C3–C8. Shared coda/household edits remain escalations.

## Explicit slots and heat-audit instructions

The manifest below gives **every individual proposed slot ID**, its source scene/node, default heated-cut text and linked JSON brief. These are proposed insertions at existing thresholds, not new scene IDs that replace old ones. Append a dedicated paragraph/node `<scene>.explicit.<n>`; keep the old source node and its saved answer targets. For existing mixed threshold/aftermath nodes put the slot between initiation and the retained aftermath, preserving legacy exit flags/resources on the same existing exit. No resource or relationship state is set by the generated text. Empty/unfilled content uses the default and still flows.

Briefs follow the Kiana example's `voice`, `scene`, `last_line`, `speakers`, `example`, `commander`, `min_lines` and `max_lines`; anatomy-dependent scenes also have `commander_variants`. Added `facts`, `source`, `slot_id`, `default_text` and `required_beats` make provenance and boundaries inspectable. Traits are not reusable native dialogue. The tagged example is a new, non-explicit staging exchange. The exact final line leads to the preserved morning/afterward or the old terminal exit. Speakers omit Commander dialogue: player speech remains choices. No sexual act is written in this sheet or the examples.

Rates below are local editorial assessments, **not independent rubric scores**: **W** = situation/voice rewrite needed; **C** = physically frank threshold but premature cut, allocate slot; **H** = adequate heated non-explicit beat, retain with its specified consequence; **A** = aftermath/partner state needs concrete carry; **N** = contextual nonsexual passage, included to prevent accidental sexualization. Each row has an exact one-line source excerpt; H/N rows deliberately report no weak passage instead of inventing a defect. For variant nodes the manifest provides a separate ID even where source text is identical. First-night versus later-night wording is history-specific, using existing night receipts, not a new attraction requirement.

Rewrite classes used in the exhaustive ledger:

- **R1, early appetite:** a hungry kiss, precise appraisal or impatient bid in the actual interrupted gaming/private-room situation; remove correctness recitals. Heat 2/3 (physical, non-explicit). Keep the partner's visible involvement or chosen absence.
- **R2, pivot:** hand/wound/claim first, both independent answers, Commander choices; heat 2/3. No generic romantic thesis, new proof device or a rescue-earned yes.
- **R3, secret:** malicious chosen concealment, risky house access, existing key/clothes discovery. Heat 3/3 at a willing threshold; explicit slot where listed. Keep exposed/cooled/closed fallout and the other lover's move.
- **R4, morning:** physical aftermath plus bandage or exact receipt; lover's frank appraisal and jealousy; next war duty interrupts. Heat 2/3, non-explicit unless a distinct return encounter already exists. Care is possessive, not redemption; shared mornings only.
- **R5, partner answer:** prices, rage, key/knife/letter and a decision; heat 1–2/3. Retain frank sex words in confrontation; no soft reassurance, disembodied present partner or new guaranteed breakup/forgiveness.
- **R6, quiet intimacy:** preserve the selected kiss/rest/company branch as non-explicit; concrete appetite or exhausted proximity can be warm without becoming a second sex scene. Heat 1–2/3; remove narrator certifications and domestic filler where present.
- **R7, ending/coda:** rare physical return, their house, current lover state and persistent scar/debt. Heat 2/3 in an actual living visit; death/loss/service endings remain their own outcomes, not sex. Retain every legacy exit's identity and mechanics.
- **R8, nonsexual boundary:** resurrection/collateral/public punishment/service is pressure, not romantic proof. No explicit slot and no seductive framing of an inert body or unwilling debt-holder.

## Ordered round-2 implementation checklist (future work only)

Finding-to-treatment map (TP audit labels retained for coordinator review):

| Finding/class | Situation or artifact | Scope disposition |
|---|---|---|
| MC-01, MC-03 | SP2: staged existing seal proof, deliberate unfinished hunt, observed dawn collection | Adopt within known effects; new decoy-enforcement mechanics escalated. |
| MC-02 | SP3: Chivarro's killing proposal and Minagho's self-interested interception | Adopt, no gratitude or moral conversion. |
| MC-04 | SP4: arrogant public reversal with actual independent correspondence | Adapt; no second forged-proof device or new bout. |
| MC-05; CHI-01 | SP4/SP5: independent invitation, existing purses/rent accounts, madam's controlled solicitation | Adopt within existing payments; no new fees; Chivarro's device remains hers. |
| MC-06 | SP4: existing patron offer burned; house named in its existing beat | Adopt; paper destruction does not cancel a seal. |
| CHI-02 | SP3/SP5: Herrax's hostile customer/leverage counter-move | Adapt within existing rivalry; no benevolent alliance or employee transfer. |
| CHI-03 | SP5/SP6: initiates her night, quarrels next day over who was told first | Adopt in existing dawn; no jealousy meter or extra forgiveness. |
| TP P1/F1; F2–4 | SP1/SP2, C1–C4: wet/dry mark, eyeless staging, inaccessible Azata host | Prose plan; shared/native implementation escalated. |
| TP F18–19; P8/F20; F21–25 | SP3/SP4/SP6: claimed-owner versus carrier, actual reunion provenance, rings debt versus scar | Preserve current repairs; collect only named debt after coordinator resolves payment/host. |
| TP P9–19/F23,F26–35 | SP4–6: solo partner state, shared mornings, physical/letter payment parity | Mirror existing histories, no new reconciliation or cost. |
| TP P20–33/F36–42 | SP6 and checklist: current reactions, one arrangement, return/pacing and fallback parity | Shared arbitration/queue work escalated; no gates or tests here. |
| TP F43–45/F48–51 | R1/R5/R8: predatory bid and self-interested performers, guest/service limits | Keep already repaired villain voice; no new protective morality. |
| TP F46–47 | `minachiv.the_unhired_evening` / `together`, sibling quiet and kiss branches | Replace mutual-intention recital with active appetite; map willing thresholds to slots. |
| TP F52–53 | Context audit of `the_paper_she_kept` / `fear`; entrance/robe/night siblings | Forced Commander speech remains future source-owner work; brief continuity forbids duplicate garment removal. |
| User heat audit, all existing physical/romantic nodes | Individual slot manifest plus full node/paragraph ledger | Every threshold, non-explicit bid, morning and partner reaction has a disposition. |

1. Record source/export baseline and existing scene/node/relationship IDs, answer positions/targets, effects and CRLF bytes. No rename, reorder or deletion; new choices append; retired branches retain their old targets behind gates. Existing ending Continue/Leave choices keep Next, Abort, Set and resource effects.
2. Coordinate shared dependencies before any source implementation: native pain selector, Ch3 Azata-only host, actual death provenance/closure, current presence adapters, Long Con eyeless siblings, Wenduag hub, household/Last Call arbitration. A historical return authorizes no fresh arrival after departure. Authored native alternates affect only earned Trickster outcomes and preserve native versions elsewhere.
3. Stage MC-01/03 inside the existing paid transfer/receipt events with the known effect only. No new test mechanic. Resolve the exact receipt sequence before describing additional enforcement; otherwise escalate that subsection. Preserve existing collateral, Favors, iron, scar and known payment histories.
4. Restore only audit-named narrative debts: name-price before all commitment siblings; bill release before a willing purchased-Chivarro invitation; missing ring payment/burial after confirmed unprepared death; rent 100/200 physical/letter parity, charge once on the existing selected dispatch/acceptance, retain existing insufficient-funds/refund behavior. No new price or unrelated prerequisite.
5. Implement SP3/SP4's partner counter-moves and registered brand-lowest-point pivot in the existing scenes; keep stance module's share/exclusive/secret answers, refusals and backdown. Mirror living/distant/dead/cellar/unknown states, both transfer provenances and letter histories. Coordinate Chivarro-owned content rather than changing her individual registered device.
6. Apply the heat ledger by class across all generated stance siblings and source copies. Insert each approved explicit slot append-only at its mapped threshold; fallback text must reach the original aftermath and exit. Do not route kept-service, owned-bill or revival text into a romantic slot. Later encounters recall the one first night; physical and letter alternatives do not duplicate it.
7. Reconcile existing continuation and Trickster outcome writers at their already accepted arrangement. Keep together/two/Minagho/Chivarro/open/friends/service distinct; letters pay/offer but never set arrival; lock only where already authorized. Carry partner refusal/exposure and current state into both ending families and shared codas.
8. Implement SP6 morning/return carries; correct every literal everyday-attendance claim to shared mornings where the actual house terms require rare visits. Keep scar/kneeling/Goat-room consequences and the killed-lover memory. No living postwar content for an unreturned sacrifice.
9. Coordinator acceptance histories: primed/unprimed spared transfer, earned plot-death restoration, deliberate-kill closure, confirmed versus unresolved Chivarro combat, prepared versus unprepared death, returned then departed partner, honest pair and both solo targets, share refusal/backdown/insistence, secret discovery physical/letter/return/unknown, scar soft retry, rent dispatch/abort/receipt, first-night versus later continuation, wet/dry pain cue, independent current companion reactions, exactly one ending/coda with a selectable exit. Keep existing readiness delays and combined delivery budget; no new shortcut gate.
10. Only the coordinator's implementation pass runs expansion/verification/tests and explicit-generation batch. This planning job executes none. No independent >=91 score is claimed.

### Outcomes that must differ

| History | Result carried to partner/household/endings/Last Call |
|---|---|
| Aid or purchase only | Available contact, existing debt; no romance inferred. |
| Pair shared acceptance | Both on-page answers; own house, rare invitation, hungry private night, actual shared dawn. |
| Honest solo; living/distant partner | Solo night is not breakup; the other's own reply/return changes the current invitation terms. |
| Exclusive demand, then backdown | Her refusal is real; sharing continues only by the existing acceptance. |
| Exclusive demand, insisted | Cooled/closed invitation, both women's reaction; no replacement night. |
| Secret, not yet exposed | Chosen affair, known risk, unknown/distant state retained; no narrator claim that partner approved. |
| Secret exposed | Key/belt/receipt confrontation, women keep each other, Commander's invitation ends; no free reconciliation. |
| Owned/service kept | Commercial coercion remains named; no sexual compliance portrayed as romantic reward. |
| Chivarro unprepared death | Actual loss, killer grief and audited ring debt; no phantom body, no scar-for-pardon. |
| Chivarro prepared substitution/purchase | Confirmation plus actual release/purchase; Sael and Herrax favor remembered; not a free resurrected lover. |
| Minagho earned plot-death return | Exact paid brand/collateral history; affection separately chosen. |
| Execution/user-ruled closure or failed unpaid return | Canon closure/death; no household/Last Call/ending body. |
| Last Call survival versus unreturned sacrifice | Living invited return only in the former; memory/loss in the latter. |

### Escalations, unimplemented proposals and risks

**ESCALATE:** All source/export work; native pain selector, Long Con, Wenduag, shared current-presence/household/Last Call code, actual kill provenance versus the old spec's overbroad revival, Ch3 host, outcome/pacing arbitration; coordinator registry remains outside permission. Ring transaction payment/host and MC-03's exact receipt enforcement need source-owner confirmation. Chivarro-only slot ownership and her independent counter-offer must be coordinated; the shared relationship is audited here, not redesigned.

**PROPOSE (not implemented):** the six situations and mapped slot insertions; MC-01–06/CHI-01–03 adaptations above; the audited single rings-paying situation. No optional new mechanic, gate, currency, reconciliation, aphrodisiac or dark Commander blackmail layer is proposed.

**RISKS:** Story.json was read as supplied, not regenerated; the ledger cites exported node IDs and source siblings, and does not certify runtime availability. Existing stance module may give several identical secret-night copies; implementers must preserve IDs while using the history's actual first/later-night wording. Current source has repaired several audit-era lines; retain strong passages instead of indiscriminate escalation of every kiss into sex. Explicit-generation continuity, anatomy variants and final independent rubric scores remain coordinator/auditor work. The planning-only hard rule overrides the boilerplate quick-gate request; the final instruction forbids committing, so this work is left uncommitted.
